**[00:15:04.680] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000110)  
The Department's overarching framework is defined by its core mandate to uphold the rule of law and serve the government's principal law agency.

**[00:15:15.080] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000111)  
In pursuit of its vision to foster a just and peaceful society rooted in transparency, accountability, fairness, and truth,

**[00:15:23.480] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000112)  
the Department executes its mission by delivering an effective, efficient, and equitable administration of justice nationwide.

**[00:15:32.600] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000113)  
The Department administers the criminal justice system through the investigation of crimes, the prosecution of offenders, and the administration of the correctional system.

**[00:15:43.600] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000114)  
Beyond criminal justice, the Department also maintains oversight over immigration, citizenship, and land titling system.

**[00:15:51.480] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000115)  
Above all,

**[00:15:53.460] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000116)  
The Department guarantees social justice by extending free legal assistance to our most vulnerable and indigent citizens.

**[00:16:01.520] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000117)  
In the next few slides, we will highlight some of the key operational accomplishments of the Department.

**[00:16:07.680] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000118)  
The Department of Justice, through the National Prosecution Service, consistently demonstrated enhanced prosecutorial output and case management efficiency over the past five years.

**[00:16:19.240] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000119)  
More significantly in 2025,

**[00:16:22.980] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000120)  
NPS

**[00:16:23.740] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000122)  
handled a heavy total caseload of 428,779 cases.

**[00:16:31.600] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000123)  
By year-end,

**[00:16:32.760] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000124)  
395,780 of these were disposed and resolved,

**[00:16:38.240] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000125)  
resulting in 99.12% of cases resolved within the prescribed 120 days,

**[00:16:44.860] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000126)  
improving upon the 99.08% accomplishment recorded in 2024.

**[00:16:50.260] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000127)  
The resolved cases include all those complaints,

**[00:16:53.460] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000128)  
under preliminary investigation, inquest, and motion for reconsideration.

**[00:16:59.280] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000129)  
Turning to court dispositions, the Department has consistently sustained high prosecution success rates over the past five years.

**[00:17:07.700] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000130)  
As shown in this slide, the Department poses upward trajectory in terms of PSR.

**[00:17:13.700] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000131)  
The PSR rose to 90.84% in 2025 from 90.14% in 2024.

**[00:17:23.460] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000132)  
This performance reflects concrete results of the Department's institutional reforms,

**[00:17:28.800] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000133)  
especially in strengthening pre-trial case buildup, tightening evidence standards, and adopting a proactive role in criminal investigation.

**[00:17:39.020] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000134)  
Moving on to our overall agency performance,

**[00:17:42.940] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000135)

**[00:17:53.460] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000137)

**[00:17:59.540] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000138)

**[00:18:06.480] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000139)

**[00:18:12.360] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000140)

**[00:18:18.580] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000141)

**[00:18:23.460] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000142)

**[00:18:28.920] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000143)

**[00:31:03.420] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000491)

**[00:31:11.180] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000492)  
which is 2.21% lower than the 316 million budget in 2026.

**[00:31:18.240] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000493)

**[00:31:25.440] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000494)  
and that 82% of all contracts will be reviewed and legal opinions will be rendered within the prescribed period.

**[00:31:31.500] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000495)  
For the Office of the Solicitor General, the 2027 recommended budget is increased to 2.14 billion from 1.9 billion in 2026

**[00:31:41.340] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000496)  
with a positive variance of 13.15% in their budget.

**[00:31:46.040] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000497)

**[00:31:51.020] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000498)  
out of which 99% are acted upon within 30 days.

**[00:31:55.240] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000499)

**[00:32:02.540] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000500)

**[00:32:08.360] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000501)

**[00:32:16.100] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000502)

**[00:32:23.080] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000503)  
will be compliant with the terms of the PPA.

**[00:32:25.240] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000504)

**[00:32:41.660] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000505)

**[00:32:47.200] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000506)  
For 2027, the PCGG targets at least 846 million of surrendered or sequestered

**[00:32:55.240] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000507)

**[00:32:58.560] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000508)

**[00:33:04.740] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000509)

**[00:33:12.040] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000510)  
With this recommended budget, the PAO will be able to achieve 83.05% favorable judgments

**[00:33:17.740] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000511)  
for its disposed cases including appeals in 2027.

**[00:33:22.240] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000512)

**[00:33:25.240] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000513)

**[00:33:29.960] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000514)

**[00:33:34.140] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000515)

**[00:33:37.800] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000516)

**[00:33:43.840] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000517)

**[00:33:58.420] DOJ Undersecretary Majken Anika S. Gran-Ong** (asr-000520)

**[00:34:34.340] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000532)

**[00:34:37.460] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000533)

**[00:34:44.720] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000535)

**[02:01:18.180] BuCor Director General Gregorio Pio P. Catapang Jr.** (asr-003538)

**[02:01:31.680] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003542)  
Especially with the residual powers of the President.

**[02:01:38.740] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003543)  
I think legally, no need for a new law for that.

**[02:01:45.240] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003544)  
Senator Ping and Secretary Vida.

**[02:01:48.620] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003545)  
An executive order will do.

**[02:01:51.000] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003546)  
Under the administrative code.

**[02:01:52.420] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003547)  
Actually, we did that under President Aquino's time.

**[02:01:55.560] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003548)  
We took away the NFA.

**[02:01:57.340] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003549)  
The NIA.

**[02:01:59.980] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003550)  
The Pesticide and Fertilizer Authority.

**[02:02:05.760] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003551)  
And the Philippine Coconut Authority.

**[02:02:07.700] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003552)  
We took it all away from the Department of Agriculture.

**[02:02:11.120] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003553)  
And put it under the office of the President.

**[02:02:13.800] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003554)  
By way of executive order.

**[02:02:16.120] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003555)  
So, an executive order can now transfer the BJMP from the DILG.

**[02:02:23.400] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003556)  
Unless there is a law.

**[02:02:25.520] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003557)  
But I don't think so.

**[02:02:26.320] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003558)  
The law is.

**[02:02:27.360] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003559)  
The administrative code.

**[02:02:29.420] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003560)  
Correct?

**[02:02:29.820] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003561)  
If Yusek Marco is more knowledgeable on the current status now.

**[02:02:36.560] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003562)  
There are already ongoing discussions on this, Your Honors.

**[02:02:39.560] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003563)  
Isa nang tanong ko.

**[02:02:40.560] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003564)  
Anong status doon sa recommendation?

**[02:02:43.020] DOJ Undersecretary Deo Marco** (asr-003565)  
With the permission of the Secretary.

**[02:02:44.680] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003566)  
Is the President positive on this?

**[02:02:48.000] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003567)  
Thank you.

**[02:02:49.340] DOJ Undersecretary Deo Marco** (asr-003568)  
With the permission of the Secretary.

**[02:02:51.600] DOJ Undersecretary Deo Marco** (asr-003569)  
Good morning, Your Honors.

**[02:02:53.160] DOJ Undersecretary Deo Marco** (asr-003570)  
At first, we were looking at two ways to do it.

**[02:02:55.600] DOJ Undersecretary Deo Marco** (asr-003571)  
First one would be through legislation.

**[02:02:57.340] DOJ Undersecretary Deo Marco** (asr-003572)  
But during the study, we figured out that this was done before through an executive order.

**[02:03:02.960] DOJ Undersecretary Deo Marco** (asr-003573)  
The only law that could probably be maybe a problem for this would be the local government code.

**[02:03:10.420] DOJ Undersecretary Deo Marco** (asr-003574)  
Because the provincial jails, according to the local government code, belongs to the provincial.

**[02:03:17.080] DOJ Undersecretary Deo Marco** (asr-003575)  
That's the only problem, Your Honors.

**[02:03:21.160] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003576)  
Yeah.

**[02:03:22.060] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003577)  
Binigyan ng staff ko, no?

**[02:03:23.560] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003578)  
Also under the Government Optimization Act.

**[02:03:26.360] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003579)  
No?

**[02:03:27.340] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003580)  
Pwede rin mag-reorganize yung Pangulo.

**[02:03:29.980] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003581)  
So maraming available na pieces of legislation.

**[02:03:33.080] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003582)  
No longer legislation, but batas.

**[02:03:35.280] DOJ Undersecretary Deo Marco** (asr-003583)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:03:35.980] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003584)  
So maybe you can work around itong dinidiscuss natin.

**[02:03:40.020] DOJ Undersecretary Deo Marco** (asr-003585)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:03:40.540] DOJ Undersecretary Deo Marco** (asr-003586)  
The present status right now is we have already formed a technical working group with the DILG, with the BJMP.

**[02:03:46.720] DOJ Undersecretary Deo Marco** (asr-003587)  
And by the end of this month, we should be done with the draft EO already.

**[02:03:50.620] DOJ Undersecretary Deo Marco** (asr-003588)  
Thank you.

**[02:03:51.020] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003589)  
What is the exact provision in the local government code?

**[02:03:55.080] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003590)  
Would you have it?

**[02:03:56.260] DOJ Undersecretary Deo Marco** (asr-003591)  
I'll look it up.

**[02:03:57.340] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003592)  
As you, before, we can proceed with the other items, but later on, we'll go back to you.

**[02:04:02.340] DOJ Undersecretary Deo Marco** (asr-003593)  
Thank you, Your Honors.

**[02:04:04.120] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003594)  
Wala na akong.

**[02:04:04.760] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003595)  
Alas dosi na, Mr. Chairman.

**[02:04:06.420] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003596)  
Pauwi na natin sila.

**[02:04:08.740] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003597)  
Meron lang akong ilang katanungan.

**[02:04:10.900] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003598)  
For the record lang, Senator Ping, we were able to collate.

**[02:04:16.000] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003599)  
The department was able to remit to the Bureau of Treasury 18,715,924,000.

**[02:04:24.020] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003600)  
But what was released under...

**[02:04:27.340] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003601)

**[02:04:36.820] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003602)  
That's based on that.

**[02:04:42.720] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003604)

**[02:04:45.360] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003605)  
Maliit.

**[02:04:46.260] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-003606)  
Yes, sir.

Sir, pahabol ko lang po. Pasensya na po.

**[02:04:49.380] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003608)  
Go ahead, Director.

**[02:04:50.220] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003609)  
Meron po rin kaming request saan ng restatement doon sa proposal namin.

**[02:04:54.340] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003610)  
This will help yung aming mga...

**[02:04:57.340] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003611)  
mga agents po.

**[02:04:59.020] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003612)  
I'm requesting for a budget for the increase of their hazard pay.

**[02:05:03.380] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003613)  
Ngayon po kasi they're just getting P4,000 a month na hazard pay

**[02:05:07.200] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003614)  
which is no longer at par with other law enforcement group.

**[02:05:10.460] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003615)  
Baka po pwedeng gawin P15,000.

**[02:05:12.020] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003616)  
This will increase po about P97 million a year po na madagdag.

**[02:05:16.120] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003617)  
Which will boost the morale of our law enforcement po,

**[02:05:19.020] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003618)  
ng mga agents po natin.

**[02:05:20.200] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003619)  
Salamat po.

**[02:05:22.380] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003620)  
Wala sa NBI modernization law yan?

**[02:05:25.940] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003621)  
Tinatanong na, Senator Ping.

**[02:05:26.640] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003622)  
Actually, nakalagay po.

**[02:05:27.180] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003623)  
Nakalagay po na wala pong nakalagay na provisions na gano'n.

**[02:05:31.440] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003624)  
Pero we are actually requesting for that para lang po sana yung ikot po ng tao.

**[02:05:37.320] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003625)  
We only have about 370 agents po sa buong Pilipinas po.

**[02:05:41.880] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003626)  
Ganun lang po karami.

**[02:05:42.780] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003627)  
Pero nagsulat na po kami sa DBM to increase the number of our agents.

**[02:05:47.920] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003628)  
Kasi under the modernization,

**[02:05:50.760] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003629)  
ang minimum po na requirement na agents namin per region at per district.

**[02:05:54.740] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003630)  
When you say district, yung province po, the 82 provinces.

**[02:05:57.180] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003631)  
Now, 6 dapat per region at per district.

**[02:06:02.040] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003632)  
Ganyan po, we only have 370 po.

**[02:06:04.140] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003633)  
Director Melvin, kindly check again.

**[02:06:06.560] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003634)  
Kasi baka may naliligaw na provision para budget na lang yung problema.

**[02:06:11.880] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003635)  
Baka mayroong provision doon na related na pwedeng lagyan ng budget.

**[02:06:16.960] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003636)  
Pakireview na lang ulit yung NBI modernization.

**[02:06:20.400] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003637)  
Kasi kapapasa lang yan.

**[02:06:22.720] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003638)  
Baka naman mayroong naliligaw doon na nabanggit yung hazard.

**[02:06:25.840] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003639)  
Sige po.

**[02:06:27.180] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003640)  
Nagsulat na lang po namin sa inyo po.

**[02:06:28.460] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003641)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[02:06:29.960] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003642)  
Address it to the chairman of the committee.

**[02:06:31.900] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003643)  
Ito pala meron oh.

**[02:06:32.920] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003644)  
There you go.

**[02:06:34.340] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003645)  
Go ahead sir.

**[02:06:35.040] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003646)  
Section 10.

**[02:06:38.000] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003647)  
Benefits and privileges.

**[02:06:40.920] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003648)  
Paragraph E.

**[02:06:41.940] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003649)  
Hazard pay.

**[02:06:43.880] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003650)  
Subject to pertinent laws and rules.

**[02:06:45.940] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003651)  
Meaning, pag sinabing subject to pertinent laws, gaayon.

**[02:06:50.740] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003652)  
Di ba?

**[02:06:51.420] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003653)  
So hindi na kailangan ng legislation.

**[02:06:53.300] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003654)  
It's already there.

**[02:06:56.240] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003655)  
Yes sir.

**[02:06:57.180] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003656)  
That's why we're requesting po for the...

**[02:06:58.980] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003657)  
Kaya, mag-usap pala kayo ng chairman.

**[02:07:01.100] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003658)  
Opo.

**[02:07:01.900] NBI Director Melvin Matibag** (asr-003659)  
97 million po yung computation namin for everyone po.

**[02:07:05.480] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003660)  
Please address it to the chairman of the committee just through me.

**[02:07:09.060] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003661)  
Chairman through the vice chairman.

**[02:07:11.680] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003662)  
Thank you.

**[02:07:12.500] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003663)  
Go ahead sir.

**[02:07:14.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003664)  
Siguro, just a few more points sa Mr. Chair.

**[02:07:18.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003665)  
Siguro, dahil ka-ask to us, Commissioner Joel.

**[02:07:23.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003666)  
Viado sa BI.

**[02:07:24.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003667)  
Okay.

**[02:07:25.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003668)  
Yung pong...

**[02:07:27.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003669)  
e-gates and Border Management System.

**[02:07:29.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003670)  
Kasi budget eh.

**[02:07:30.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003671)  
Kaya I have to ask.

**[02:07:32.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003672)  
As early as

**[02:07:32.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003673)  
2017, Bureau of Immigration has been receiving allocation for its Border Management Information System.

**[02:07:39.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003674)  
From 2017 to 2026, the project has received a total allocation of almost 10 billion. For 2027,

**[02:07:46.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003675)  
Allocation of BMIS is just 524 million.

**[02:07:51.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003676)  
Pero through the years, malaki na rin ito.

**[02:07:54.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003677)  
2024, 2.6 billion.

**[02:07:58.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003678)  
2025, 3.8 billion.

**[02:08:01.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003679)  
2026, 1.8 billion.

**[02:08:04.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003680)  
Just like to ask, medyo malaki kasi ito.

**[02:08:08.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003681)  
But what are the components so that everybody will understand of this project aside from the expansion of the e-gates in our international ports?

**[02:08:16.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003682)  
Ano po ito?

**[02:08:16.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003683)  
For convenience lang?

**[02:08:17.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003684)  
Or does it include the software, lahat po ng information system?

**[02:08:23.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003685)  
So probably can...

**[02:08:24.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003686)  
You can expound, Commissioner Joel.

**[02:08:27.940] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003687)  
Yes.

**[02:08:28.320] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003688)  
Thank you very much po, Senator Ejercito.

**[02:08:31.480] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003689)  
With the permission of our Honorable Secretary.

**[02:08:33.580] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003690)  
Ang components po ng e-gates project po namin, which aims to not only strengthen border control but also modernize the primary immigration processing,

**[02:08:46.000] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003691)  
accelerate clearances, prevent fraud, and streamline the immigration processing through biometric verification.

**[02:08:53.520] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003692)  
Ang components po niyan are the following.

**[02:08:54.740] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003693)  
First, biometrics capture device in manned inspection counters.

**[02:09:00.280] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003694)  
Ang description po nito, it is a self-contained device at manned counters, which will capture the traveler's biometric data, such as fingerprints and facial images, with seamless software integration for operation.

**[02:09:17.040] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003695)  
Number two po, yung hardware po ng e-gates.

**[02:09:20.080] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003696)  
Yan po yung nakikita po natin.

**[02:09:21.500] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003697)  
These are automated self-service gates.

**[02:09:24.740] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003698)  
These are the gates that allow eligible travelers to clear immigration without going through a manned counter.

**[02:09:31.800] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003699)  
Ito pong e-gates, they read the traveler's passport, they capture live biometrics, they verify the person's identity against the passport, they check the regulatory records, and open the gates if all checks are passed.

**[02:09:46.360] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003700)  
Kasama rin po dito ang automated biometric identification system, o yung tinatawag po nating ABIS.

**[02:09:52.000] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003701)  
It's the central system that stores...

**[02:09:54.740] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003702)  
It's the central system that stores and matches face and fingerprint data for real-time identity verification and fraud detection.

**[02:10:02.200] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003703)  
Meron din pong e-gates monitoring station.

**[02:10:05.220] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003704)  
Ito po ang workstations used by immigration officers to monitor the status and performance of all e-gates in real-time.

**[02:10:13.800] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003705)  
The system shows whether each gate is operational, in use, or requires assistance, allowing officers to respond quickly to any issues.

**[02:10:22.300] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003706)  
Andyan din po ang border control data.

**[02:10:24.720] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003708)  
exchange platform, or BCDEP.

**[02:10:28.480] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003709)  
The secure platform that enables safe, real-time data sharing among border control systems and agencies.

**[02:10:36.480] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003710)  
Andyan din po ang administrative back-end system.

**[02:10:39.420] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003711)  
The management system for monitoring usage, performance, and configuration of e-gates and related components.

**[02:10:46.180] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003712)  
Meron din pong document verification software.

**[02:10:48.760] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003713)  
It displays all verification results performed on the passport within the immigration...

**[02:10:54.720] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003714)  
...officer's interface.

**[02:10:57.440] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003715)  
Nakaka-indicate po siya ng any detected anomalies or inconsistencies.

**[02:11:01.200] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003716)  
Meron din pong network requirements.

**[02:11:03.920] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003717)  
Kailangan po kasi yung communications infrastructure maigi.

**[02:11:07.880] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003718)  
Nandun po yung switchers, routers, cabling, tsaka yung Wi-Fi that can securely connect all e-gate components.

**[02:11:14.840] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003719)  
Meron din pong server requirements na kailangan pong kasusan.

**[02:11:18.480] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003720)  
The servers host and run core e-gate systems to handle transactions reliably...

**[02:11:24.720] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003721)  
...and support future growth.

**[02:11:27.140] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003722)  
Bangsan po po ang system integration.

**[02:11:29.180] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003723)  
It will connect all e-gates and biometric systems with the border control information system...

**[02:11:35.300] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003724)  
...and other databases for real-time checks and smooth passenger flow.

**[02:11:40.180] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003725)  
Meron din pong backup and disaster recovery.

**[02:11:43.320] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003726)  
Yan po yung kung mag-down yung system, dapat po may backup.

**[02:11:46.760] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003727)  
The data backups and redundant systems aim to protect information and restore operations quickly.

**[02:11:54.720] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003728)  
After failures or disruptions.

**[02:11:57.520] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003729)  
Meron din pong kailangan kasusan na support, maintenance, and warranty.

**[02:12:02.900] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003730)  
For ongoing technical support, preventive maintenance, and repairs to keep all e-gates components running reliably.

**[02:12:11.940] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003731)  
And last but not the least, anyan po yung training or structured training for the officers and staff...

**[02:12:18.140] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003732)  
...on operating, monitoring, and maintaining the e-gates and associated systems.

**[02:12:23.220] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003733)  
So we have to consider...

**[02:12:24.720] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003734)  
...your honors, na hindi lang po yung hardware ang kailangan pong paglaanan ng budget.

**[02:12:31.180] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003735)  
Andiyan po ang isang system na kailangan tumakbo not only in the airports in Metro Manila...

**[02:12:37.740] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003736)  
...but interconnected with all the other airports in the whole Philippines.

**[02:12:41.640] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003737)  
Yan po.

**[02:12:42.400] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003738)  
Chair Chairman, ang facial identification or facial recognition, yung requirement ninyo?

**[02:12:48.860] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003739)  
Facial recognition, yung requirement.

**[02:12:50.080] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003740)  
Recognition, mahal yan.

**[02:12:52.280] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003741)  
We have been working, your honor...

**[02:12:54.560] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003742)  
...with the...

**[02:12:54.720] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003743)  
...within the budget, kung ano po ang naibigay sa amin pong budget po ng ating pong kapulungan.

**[02:13:00.320] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003744)  
Yun po, we're working within that po.

**[02:13:02.600] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003745)  
So ang stages po niyan, ang first phase po, 1.9 billion, but we were able to lower that to 1.5 billion po.

**[02:13:12.700] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003746)  
And secondly po, Your Honor, yung phase 2 po ng proyekto namin is around [unclear].

**[02:13:23.740] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003747)  
1 billion 347 million 57 thousand.

**[02:13:29.340] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003748)  
Hindi ko kasanayin na magbasa ng napakaraming...

**[02:13:32.120] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003749)  
Lagi mo inisip yung milyon.

**[02:13:33.820] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003750)  
Lagi, laging, laging, ano po.

**[02:13:36.060] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003751)  
And phase 3 po ng proyekto is for 2026, 536,072,000, your honors.

**[02:13:45.420] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003752)  
Yan po.

**[02:13:46.140] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003753)  
Kasi basically, di ba, pagka facial identification, ang tanong lang, sino to?

**[02:13:51.300] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003754)  
Pero pagka facial recognition, may matching.

**[02:13:53.700] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003755)  
May matching pa ito, di ba?

**[02:13:54.520] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003756)  
Yes, po, your honor.

**[02:13:55.680] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003757)  
Kaya po, meron po kaming biometric capturing systems po na binili.

**[02:13:59.300] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003758)  
Not only the hardware, but meron pong biometric capturing devices po na binili, your honor.

**[02:14:05.080] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003759)  
Kasi yan po ang crucial.

**[02:14:07.000] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003760)  
Meron pa nga pong instances, your honor, na meron pong airports na maaring hindi kailangan ng e-gates because of the passenger traffic,

**[02:14:15.120] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003761)  
pero kailangan ng biometric identification system.

**[02:14:18.040] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003762)  
Yan po.

**[02:14:19.180] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003763)  
Sa Southeast Asia, tayo na lang ba yung walang facial recognition?

**[02:14:24.520] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003764)  
Hindi ko po masabi sa buong Southeast Asia, your honor, but I've been to different airports all over Southeast Asia and Asia.

**[02:14:32.440] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003765)  
Nakita ko na po yan sa, of course, Singapore, ang Indonesia, sa Bali, ang dami po nilang mga e-gates, ang Japan, of course, and Korea.

**[02:14:42.420] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003766)  
Yan po. Marami po talagang mga bansa, your honors, na gumagamit.

**[02:14:45.260] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003767)  
Kindly walk us through, no? Pagka meron na tayong facial recognition technology.

**[02:14:50.220] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003768)  
Yes, your honor.

**[02:14:52.780] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003769)  
Anong...

**[02:14:53.260] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003770)  
Anong mako-cover nito?

**[02:14:54.780] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003771)  
Pagdating ng pasahero, wala na tayo yung mga boots.

**[02:14:58.880] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003772)  
Yes, your honor.

**[02:14:59.580] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003773)  
Yes, your honor.

**[02:15:00.000] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003774)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:15:00.880] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003775)  
Hanggang Pochina.

**[02:15:01.900] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003776)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:15:02.500] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003777)  
That is the ultimate objective, Your Honor.

**[02:15:04.660] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003778)  
Gaya po ng sa ibang bansa, Your Honor.

**[02:15:06.520] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003779)  
Nadadaan na lang po kayo sa Amerika po.

**[02:15:08.180] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003780)  
Ganun.

**[02:15:08.640] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003781)  
Pagdaan, nakaka-capture na po lahat.

**[02:15:10.340] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003782)  
Lahat captured na.

**[02:15:11.340] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003783)  
So, you don't need to queue.

**[02:15:14.720] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003784)  
Di ba?

**[02:15:14.880] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003785)  
Hindi ka na magka-queue.

**[02:15:15.980] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003786)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:15:17.080] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003787)  
Ganun po.

**[02:15:17.700] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003788)  
Because to which is own, you just go through.

**[02:15:21.560] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003789)  
Yes po, Your Honor.

**[02:15:22.920] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003790)  
Ang maganda po kasi sa ganitong approach, Your Honor,

**[02:15:26.140] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003791)  
yung manual counters po,

**[02:15:27.760] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003792)  
kung hindi mababawasan, matatanggal totally.

**[02:15:31.300] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003793)  
But we are adopting a step-ladder approach.

**[02:15:34.360] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003794)  
Kasi po, we have to admit,

**[02:15:36.220] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003795)  
kumbaga ho sa levels,

**[02:15:38.440] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003796)  
kumbaga ho nandito pa ho tayo sa level 1.

**[02:15:40.300] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003797)  
Hindi naman ho pwedeng agad-agad na sa level number 10 po tayo.

**[02:15:44.060] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003798)  
So, we have to go through the different processes and phases.

**[02:15:47.400] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003799)  
At ito po ang naisip namin na unang hakbang po,

**[02:15:51.460] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003800)  
patungo po doon sa ating adhikain,

**[02:15:53.740] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003801)  
na lahat e dumaan doon sa tinatawag po nating artificial intelligence.

**[02:15:57.820] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003802)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:15:58.360] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003803)  
Ito po ang...

**[02:15:59.140] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003804)  
Wala nang human intervention.

**[02:16:00.840] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003805)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:16:01.580] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003806)  
That would...

**[02:16:02.380] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003807)  
Ang problema nyo, saan nyo itatapon yung mga employees ninyo?

**[02:16:05.040] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003808)  
Your Honor, we have to consider yun po,

**[02:16:08.020] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003809)  
na we are expecting passenger traffic to really increase exponentially.

**[02:16:13.200] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003810)  
So, we still need the immigration personnel.

**[02:16:16.140] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003811)  
We also need people to man the different e-gates

**[02:16:19.700] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003812)  
and the different systems.

**[02:16:21.460] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003813)  
Ultimately po, maari pong ma-prevent siya from entering,

**[02:16:26.340] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003814)  
but...

**[02:16:27.760] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003815)  
The discretion of the immigration officers still remains.

**[02:16:30.120] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003816)  
So, kailangan pa rin po namin.

**[02:16:30.880] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003817)  
So, how much do we need for the first phase?

**[02:16:33.520] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003818)  
1.3 billion.

**[02:16:36.160] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003819)  
Na ibigay na po sa amin, Your Honor.

**[02:16:37.860] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003820)  
Ah, nandiyan na.

**[02:16:38.640] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003821)  
So, in the works na ito?

**[02:16:40.600] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003822)  
Yes po, Your Honor.

**[02:16:42.280] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003823)  
So, where are we now?

**[02:16:44.820] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003824)  
Sa ano po?

**[02:16:46.380] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003825)  
Sa phase 2 na po tayo, Your Honor.

**[02:16:49.660] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003826)  
Nasa phase 2 na po.

**[02:16:50.220] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003827)  
Nasa NEP yan?

**[02:16:51.820] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003828)  
Yes po, Your Honor.

**[02:16:54.280] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003829)  
Sufficient or not enough?

**[02:16:56.620] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003830)  
Yung nasa NEP?

**[02:17:01.500] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003831)  
Sapat na ba?

**[02:17:02.260] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003832)  
Enough na po, Your Honor.

**[02:17:04.480] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003833)  
If I may, Your Honor.

**[02:17:05.800] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003834)  
Wala na po nga, pigi-usapang budget.

**[02:17:08.560] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003835)  
Okay na po.

**[02:17:09.720] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003836)  
Your Honor.

**[02:17:10.500] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003837)  
I-adapt na lang yung NEP, ano?

**[02:17:11.800] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003838)  
Yes po.

**[02:17:12.300] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003839)  
I-adapt na lang yung NEP.

**[02:17:13.640] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003840)  
Yes po.

**[02:17:13.900] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-003841)  
Okay na kayo?

**[02:17:14.600] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003842)  
Apo.

**[02:17:15.000] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003843)  
If I may give the specific phases po, kung nasa na po.

**[02:17:18.680] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003844)  
Ang status po ng phase 1,

**[02:17:20.660] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003845)  
the procurement of the e-gates project has been completed

**[02:17:23.080] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003846)  
with a notice to proceed issued on 12 March 2026.

**[02:17:26.620] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003847)  
to the winning joint venture and bidder.

**[02:17:29.300] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003848)  
The project implementation will be undertaken

**[02:17:31.420] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003849)  
over a 9-month period from receipt of the NTP.

**[02:17:34.320] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003850)  
So from March 12, may 9-month period po.

**[02:17:37.180] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003851)  
Ang phase 2 po, Your Honors.

**[02:17:39.000] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003852)  
The technical specifications were submitted

**[02:17:40.800] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003853)  
to the Office of the Department of Justice,

**[02:17:43.120] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003854)  
Chief Information Technology Officer,

**[02:17:45.100] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003855)  
on September 8, 2026.

**[02:17:47.440] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003856)  
And it was approved on the same day.

**[02:17:49.780] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003857)  
Subsequently, the invitation to bid was posted

**[02:17:52.080] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003858)  
on September 19, 2026,

**[02:17:54.480] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003859)  
with a pre-bid conference set for

**[02:17:56.560] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003860)  
September 30, 2026, Your Honor.

**[02:17:59.160] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003861)  
Ang phase 3 po ng proyekto,

**[02:18:01.860] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003862)  
a letter directive constituting the e-gates phase 3

**[02:18:04.780] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003863)  
project management team, or PMT,

**[02:18:07.440] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003864)  
has been issued by the Office of the Commissioner

**[02:18:09.120] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003865)  
of the Bureau of Immigration,

**[02:18:10.560] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003866)  
and the drafting of the technical specifications

**[02:18:12.880] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003867)  
will commence shortly thereafter, Your Honor.

**[02:18:16.000] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003868)  
We have also done the required market scoping

**[02:18:19.380] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003869)  
as required by BI Memorandum No. 2025-132

**[02:18:24.680] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003870)  
in line with the new government's

**[02:18:26.560] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003871)  
Procurement Act.

**[02:18:28.060] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003872)  
So dinaanam po namin yung pag-scope po

**[02:18:30.320] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003873)  
sa merkado as a preliminary step

**[02:18:33.280] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003874)  
in strategic procurement planning, Your Honor.

**[02:19:26.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003898)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:19:29.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003900)  
So, ang component pa ito, kaya mahal,

**[02:19:31.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003901)  
it's not just for convenience

**[02:19:33.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003902)

**[02:19:34.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003903)

**[02:19:36.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003904)

**[02:19:39.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003906)

Yes, po.

**[02:19:52.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003911)

**[02:19:54.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003912)

Kaya po, Your Honor.

**[02:20:01.320] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-003916)

**[02:20:07.440] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003917)

**[02:20:08.740] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003918)

**[02:20:14.320] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003920)

**[02:20:34.620] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003929)

**[02:22:24.440] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003987)

ang immigration officers po natin

**[02:22:37.880] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003993)

**[02:22:39.880] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003994)

**[02:22:45.020] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003996)

**[02:22:49.920] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003998)

**[02:22:52.540] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-003999)

**[02:22:58.200] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004002)

**[02:23:04.800] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004004)

**[02:23:06.140] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004005)

**[02:23:11.140] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004007)

**[02:23:19.140] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004009)

**[02:23:20.240] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004010)

**[02:23:21.240] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004011)  
ng

**[02:23:22.380] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004012)

**[02:23:24.380] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004013)

**[02:23:26.380] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004014)

**[02:23:32.380] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004015)  
ang kinalakihan po natin nun,

**[02:23:35.380] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004016)  
7,100 islands ang Pilipinas.

**[02:23:37.380] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004017)

**[02:23:39.380] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004018)

**[02:23:44.380] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004019)

**[02:23:49.380] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004020)

**[02:24:00.220] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004023)

Yung arrival.

**[02:24:59.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004042)

**[02:25:01.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004043)

**[02:25:03.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004045)  
Yung ang problema kasi, yung departure,

**[02:25:06.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004046)

**[02:25:09.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004047)

**[02:25:12.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004048)

**[02:25:16.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004049)

**[02:25:19.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004050)

**[02:25:21.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004051)

**[02:25:26.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004052)

**[02:25:27.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004053)

**[02:25:29.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004054)

**[02:25:35.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004055)

**[02:25:39.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004056)

**[02:25:41.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004057)  
So, can you elaborate?

**[02:25:44.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004058)  
Yes.

**[02:25:45.220] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004059)  
Meron po kaming nakaset po ng mga,

**[02:25:47.220] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004060)  
naka-set po ng mga schedule pagdating po sa kung sino po ang pwedeng lumabas at maglabas-masok using the e-gates.

**[02:25:55.220] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004061)  
Noong ano po, noong December 2025, noong una po nating nilaunch po itong proyekto na to,

**[02:26:00.220] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004062)  
Filipinos po ang makakagamit upon arrival.

**[02:26:05.220] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004063)  
Noong February 2026 po, mga OFWs po natin, mga kababayan po nating OFWs, ang pwede na pong makagamit sa departure.

**[02:26:14.220] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004064)  
Pagdating naman po ng May 2026,

**[02:26:16.220] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004065)  
yung mga temporary visitors eligible under the, or admitted under the, tinatawag po nating EO408,

**[02:26:24.220] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004066)  
yung mga eligible countries with the 30-day visa-free arrangement,

**[02:26:29.220] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004067)  
makakagamit na rin po sila sa departure.

**[02:26:32.220] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004068)  
Pagdating naman po noong June 2026, ang foreign nationals under the Balikbayanan Program,

**[02:26:39.220] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004069)  
makakagamit po sila sa departure.

**[02:26:42.220] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004070)  
At yun pong non-first-time travelers.

**[02:26:46.220] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004073)  
Kabila sa Pilipinos, departure din po noong June 2026.

**[02:26:50.220] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004074)  
At yun pong mga registered po sa Commission on Filipinos Overseas.

**[02:26:54.220] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004075)  
Sa first quarter of 2027, ang schedule po natin,

**[02:26:59.220] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004076)  
upon arrival, ang other eligible foreign nationals can use the E-gates.

**[02:27:05.220] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004077)  
Pagdating naman po ng second quarter ng 2027, other eligible foreign nationals can now use the departure gates.

**[02:27:14.220] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004078)  
Yan po ang schedule, Your Honor.

**[02:27:16.240] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004079)  
And tama po kayo pagdating po sa mga kababayan po natin na aalis,

**[02:27:22.220] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004080)  
we have to comply with the departure formalities na tinatawag po

**[02:27:26.900] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004081)  
as set under the Interagency Council Against Trafficking

**[02:27:30.300] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004082)  
of which the Bureau of Immigration is one of the members.

**[02:27:33.020] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004083)  
We have to consider, Your Honors, that the Philippines is uniquely situated

**[02:27:38.300] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004084)  
in the sense that ang border control po natin does not only pertain to those arriving

**[02:27:43.200] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004085)  
but ang mga kababayan po natin ay piniprotektahan po natin

**[02:27:47.480] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004086)  
bago po sila umalis ng bansa just to ensure that they are safely,

**[02:27:53.300] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004087)  
they safely not only arrive but they safely work in the countries

**[02:27:57.320] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004088)  
where they intend to be employed.

**[02:27:59.340] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004089)  
Kaya po meron po mga proseso po kaming dinadaanan para humalaman

**[02:28:03.900] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004090)  
kung ano po ang potensyal ng kababayan po natin na matatraffic.

**[02:28:08.920] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004091)  
Um, ano po eh, it's unfortunate na ang mga perpetrators po

**[02:28:17.180] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004092)  
nakakaisip po ng ano, ng iba't ibang mga kumbaga gimmick po

**[02:28:20.740] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004093)  
para ho makapagpalabas po ng mga tao papunta ibang bansa.

**[02:28:30.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004094)  
Also, um, we received information, I think this was also asked sa house,

**[02:28:35.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004095)  
to sa hearing, yung sa, you're in the prior,

**[02:28:38.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004096)

**[02:28:44.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004097)

**[02:28:50.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004098)

**[02:28:58.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004100)

**[02:29:01.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004101)

**[02:29:08.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004102)

**[02:29:14.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004103)

**[02:29:19.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004104)  
Sige, Commissioner Joel, please.

**[02:29:20.620] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004105)  
Thank you very much po. Again, sir, to your ears ito.

**[02:29:23.160] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004106)  
I would like to, to make it clear at this point po.

**[02:29:26.580] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004107)  
Ah, yung Civil Aviation and Immigration Security Services program po,

**[02:29:31.220] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004108)  
or CAIS na tinatawag, ah, it remains under review.

**[02:29:34.500] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004109)  
And no final decision, ah, has been made concerning this project.

**[02:29:38.920] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004110)  
We support the modernization of our border security system,

**[02:29:43.840] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004111)  
but, ah, it should not be interpreted as an automatic approval

**[02:29:47.080] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004112)  
of the, this particular proposal or its terms.

commercial terms.

**[02:30:19.280] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004127)

**[02:30:23.360] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004128)

**[02:30:32.160] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004131)

**[02:30:35.560] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004132)

**[02:30:37.200] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004133)

**[02:30:54.960] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004141)

Initially po,

**[02:30:58.200] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004143)

Now, in the next 20 years,

**[02:31:09.340] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004147)  
ang estimate po is

**[02:31:10.680] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004148)

**[02:31:15.040] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004150)  
For the hardware upgrade,

**[02:31:16.820] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004151)

**[02:31:20.100] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004152)

**[02:31:23.400] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004153)  
Ang objective po is to move

**[02:31:25.880] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004154)

**[02:31:26.880] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004155)

**[02:31:29.120] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004156)  
and reactive screening approach

**[02:31:31.100] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004157)

**[02:31:36.780] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004158)  
We have to contend po

**[02:31:38.200] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004159)

**[02:31:40.300] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004160)

**[02:31:42.100] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004161)

**[02:31:44.500] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004162)  
aliases,

**[02:31:45.720] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004163)  
synthetic identities,

**[02:31:47.000] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004164)  
human trafficking,

**[02:31:48.060] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004165)  
terrorism,

**[02:31:48.660] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004166)  
and other forms of transnational crime.

**[02:31:51.260] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004167)  
And this system will enable the Bureau

**[02:31:53.860] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004168)  
to identify

**[02:31:55.760] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004169)

**[02:31:56.880] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004170)  
low-risk travelers earlier,

**[02:31:58.880] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004171)

**[02:32:00.880] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004172)  
while allowing legitimate,

**[02:32:02.880] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004173)

**[02:32:04.880] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004174)

**[02:32:05.880] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004175)  
Ngayon po,

**[02:32:06.880] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004176)  
ang konsepto po nito is

**[02:32:21.880] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004182)

Yan po yung konsepto nung

**[02:32:26.720] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004184)  
kais, Your Honor.

will integrate and

**[02:36:08.720] BI Commissioner Joel Anthony M. Viado** (asr-004272)

**[02:41:30.720] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004385)

**[02:41:47.720] DOJ Secretary Fredderick A. Vida** (asr-004388)

**[02:41:55.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004389)

**[02:41:59.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004390)

**[02:42:01.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004391)

2026 levels.

**[02:42:47.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004402)

**[02:42:57.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004403)  
If I may ask, PG Dong, who's...

**[02:43:00.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004404)  
PG Dong, lagi naman itong suki dito.

**[02:43:04.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004405)  
So, aside from the value of testimony and evidence they can provide, do you consider the extent na kanilang participation sa krimen, kung magkano ang kanilang ibabalik?

**[02:43:13.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004406)  
So, siguro, PG Dong, please.

**[02:43:15.720] Prosecutor General Richard Anthony Fadullon** (asr-004407)  
Yes, with the permission of the Secretary, Mr. Chair, yung doon muna sa issue po kung ano yung qualifications, kailangan po, hindi po lahat ng tao na nag-a-apply sa program are automatically admitted.

**[02:43:28.720] Prosecutor General Richard Anthony Fadullon** (asr-004408)  
There are certain qualifications among them. Number one, that the person, well, the case involves a grave, one where the penalty is a grave penalty.

**[02:43:38.720] Prosecutor General Richard Anthony Fadullon** (asr-004409)  
Number two, it also entails na yung sasabihin ng tao is substantially relevant, meron siyang kahalagahan doon sa kaso, and it can be corroborated on most of its material points.

**[02:43:52.720] Prosecutor General Richard Anthony Fadullon** (asr-004410)  
So, lastly, hindi siya yung pinaka-guilty doon sa kaso.

**[02:43:58.720] Prosecutor General Richard Anthony Fadullon** (asr-004411)  
Sa usapin naman po nung pagsasoli, hindi po yan nakasaad sa ating batas. It's not categorically stated that there is restitution.

**[02:44:08.720] Prosecutor General Richard Anthony Fadullon** (asr-004412)  
However, during po, kung halimbawa, after the evaluation at nakita na itong potential applicant is qualified, then ang mangyayari po is magkakaroon po sila ng kasunduan, a memorandum of agreement that will be signed between the applicant and the Secretary of Justice.

**[02:44:26.720] Prosecutor General Richard Anthony Fadullon** (asr-004413)  
Doon po, meron pong mga kondisyon o meron pong mga kasunduan na maaaring ilahad. At doon na po papasok yung usapin kung meron po tayong mga makukuha o meron silang isusoli bilang bahagi nung kanilang pag-a-apply po o pag-a-apply nila sa ating programa.

**[02:44:50.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004414)  
May madadagdag pa pa tayo, Sek? Siguro quickly, dahil marami nagtatanong pa rito sa flood control.

**[02:44:56.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004415)  
Sa mga makakasuhan na mga contractors,

**[02:45:00.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004416)  
congressmen, kasi po, pasensya na po, pero yung cong-tractors, marami rito, ito yung mga nagsimula ng modus eh.

**[02:45:08.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004417)

**[02:45:20.800] Prosecutor General Richard Anthony Fadullon** (asr-004418)  
Well, sir, yung tulad po na sinabi ni Secretary of Justice, marami hung variables eh.

**[02:45:25.600] Prosecutor General Richard Anthony Fadullon** (asr-004419)  
Kasi even during the time when the cases are already filed in court,

**[02:45:30.000] Prosecutor General Richard Anthony Fadullon** (asr-004420)

**[02:45:35.200] Prosecutor General Richard Anthony Fadullon** (asr-004421)  
And as a consequence, they are being admitted or asked to be admitted sa program.

**[02:45:39.620] Prosecutor General Richard Anthony Fadullon** (asr-004422)  
So yan po eh, depende sa kung ano po ang may dadagdag o may tutulong ng testimonya.

**[02:45:45.300] Prosecutor General Richard Anthony Fadullon** (asr-004423)  
Insofar as the other cases involving yung sa flood control,

**[02:45:50.060] Prosecutor General Richard Anthony Fadullon** (asr-004424)  
alam ko po marami pa pong mga kaso, marami na hung kasong na-file.

**[02:45:53.480] Prosecutor General Richard Anthony Fadullon** (asr-004425)

**[02:45:58.540] Prosecutor General Richard Anthony Fadullon** (asr-004426)  
At hinahawakan din po ng ating...

**[02:46:00.000] Prosecutor General Richard Anthony Fadullon** (asr-004427)

**[02:46:02.440] Prosecutor General Richard Anthony Fadullon** (asr-004428)

**[02:46:06.520] Prosecutor General Richard Anthony Fadullon** (asr-004429)

**[02:46:11.720] Prosecutor General Richard Anthony Fadullon** (asr-004430)  
Ito po ay patuloy dahil hindi po nagtatapos doon sa mga naisampang kaso lang.

**[02:46:16.580] Prosecutor General Richard Anthony Fadullon** (asr-004431)  
Patuloy po na nangangalap ng ebidensya din po ang National Bureau of Investigation

**[02:46:20.440] Prosecutor General Richard Anthony Fadullon** (asr-004432)  
para matingnan po yung liability and who else can be held accountable.

**[02:46:27.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004433)  
Okay, thank you.

**[02:46:28.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004434)

**[02:46:37.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004436)

**[02:48:39.120] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-004465)
